Good improvement! The explicit full-adder generate gives a different synthesis result: cost 2,792,215 (from 2,833,070). Area went up slightly (4343 vs 4308) but delay dropped (642.9 vs 657.6). Let me now try further optimizations on this base.

Let me try reducing the de-skew registers by doing the CPA after the de-skew chain (only the last column needs CPA, others have de-skew registers that can hold the carry-save form):

Wait, I already tried that (v9) and it was worse because it doubles the de-skew registers. Let me instead try **removing the de-skew registers and doing the CPA combinationally at the output**. The de-skew is needed to align columns, but what if I absorb the de-skew into the valid pipeline? No, that doesn't work because different columns need different delays.

Let me try **narrowing the de-skew registers**. The CPA output is 19 bits, but the upper bits are sign extension. Can I use fewer bits in the de-skew chain?

Actually, the de-skew chain carries the CPA result (19 bits). The CPA result's upper bits can be non-trivial (not just sign extension) because the sum of cs+cc can have carries. So I need all 19 bits.

Let me try a different approach: **move the CPA to before the de-skew but use a ripple-carry structure that Yosys can optimize differently**:
  -> create_file(content="// v19 base with explicit ripple-carry CPA at output\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter AW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  rst,\n    input\t\t  ld,\n    input\t\t  en,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,\n    input [AW-1:0]\t  ps_north,\n    input [AW-1:0]\t  pc_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,\n    output reg [AW-1:0]  ps_south,\n    output reg [AW-1:0]  pc_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n   wire [AW-1:0] prod_ext = {{(AW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   wire [AW-1:0] sum_out;\n   wire [AW-1:0] carry_out;\n   genvar k;\n   generate\n      for (k = 0; k < AW; k = k + 1) begin : fa\n         assign sum_out[k]   = ps_north[k] ^ pc_north[k] ^ prod_ext[k];\n         assign carry_out[k] = (ps_north[k] & pc_north[k]) | (ps_north[k] & prod_ext[k]) | (pc_north[k] & prod_ext[k]);\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) begin\n         w <= w_north;\n      end\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east   <= a_west;\n         ps_south <= sum_out;\n         pc_south <= {carry_out[AW-2:0], 1'b0};\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter AW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tld,\n    input\t\ten,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\n    output [N*AW-1:0]\tc_bot_s,\n    output [N*AW-1:0]\tc_bot_c\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]  a_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [AW-1:0]  ps_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [AW-1:0]  pc_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]  w_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign ps_v[0][j] = {AW{1'b0}};\n         assign pc_v[0][j] = {AW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]  = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot_s[j*AW +: AW] = ps_v[N][j];\n         assign c_bot_c[j*AW +: AW] = pc_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .AW(AW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .ps_north   (ps_v[i][j]),\n               .pc_north   (pc_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .ps_south   (ps_v[i+1][j]),\n               .pc_south   (pc_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam INTW = 19;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*INTW-1:0] c_bot_s, c_bot_c;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .AW(INTW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left),\n      .c_bot_s(c_bot_s), .c_bot_c(c_bot_c));\n\n   // De-skew with explicit ripple-carry CPA\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [INTW-1:0] cs = c_bot_s[j*INTW +: INTW];\n         wire [INTW-1:0] cc = c_bot_c[j*INTW +: INTW];\n\n         // Ripple-carry adder for CPA\n         wire [INTW-1:0] cpa_sum;\n         wire [INTW:0] cpa_carry;\n         assign cpa_carry[0] = 1'b0;\n         assign cpa_sum[0] = cs[0] ^ cc[0];\n         assign cpa_carry[1] = cs[0] & cc[0];\n         // For simplicity, use + operator - Yosys will optimize\n         wire [INTW-1:0] cpa = cs + cc;\n\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-INTW){cpa[INTW-1]}}, cpa};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [INTW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cpa;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-INTW){dr[D-1][INTW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v20.v')

Same result with target_delay=400. Let me clean up v20 by removing the dead ripple-carry code and try a few more optimizations. Let me also try using `MAJI` (majority gate) for the carry instead of the AND-OR expression, which Yosys might map differently:
  -> create_file(content="// Cleaned v20: explicit FA with MAJ for carry, no dead code\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter AW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  rst,\n    input\t\t  ld,\n    input\t\t  en,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,\n    input [AW-1:0]\t  ps_north,\n    input [AW-1:0]\t  pc_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,\n    output reg [AW-1:0]  ps_south,\n    output reg [AW-1:0]  pc_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n   wire [AW-1:0] prod_ext = {{(AW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   wire [AW-1:0] sum_out;\n   wire [AW-1:0] carry_out;\n   genvar k;\n   generate\n      for (k = 0; k < AW; k = k + 1) begin : fa\n         // Full adder: sum = a ^ b ^ c, carry = majority(a,b,c)\n         assign sum_out[k]   = ps_north[k] ^ pc_north[k] ^ prod_ext[k];\n         assign carry_out[k] = (ps_north[k] & pc_north[k]) |\n                               (ps_north[k] & prod_ext[k]) |\n                               (pc_north[k] & prod_ext[k]);\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) begin\n         w <= w_north;\n      end\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east   <= a_west;\n         ps_south <= sum_out;\n         pc_south <= {carry_out[AW-2:0], 1'b0};\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter AW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tld,\n    input\t\ten,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\n    output [N*AW-1:0]\tc_bot_s,\n    output [N*AW-1:0]\tc_bot_c\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]  a_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [AW-1:0]  ps_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [AW-1:0]  pc_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]  w_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*INTW-1:0] c_bot_s, c_bot_c;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .AW(INTW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left),\n      .c_bot_s(c_bot_s), .c_bot_c(c_bot_c));\n\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; [ASSISTANT]
Good - removing the dead code gave a tiny improvement: 2,780,232 (from 2,791,646). This is our new best.

Let me now try a few more things. Let me try making the de-skew registers narrower - the CPA result needs 19 bits, but the sign extension to 32 bits happens at output. What if I use only 16 bits in the de-skew (the lower 16 bits of the CPA) and handle the sign separately?

Actually, let me try a different approach: **use a 17-bit internal accumulator**. Wait, we need 19. But what about using 16-bit carry-save internally and handling the upper bits differently?

Let me try another idea: **merge the product into the carry-save tree as a 4:2 compressor**. Instead of computing the full product first and then doing CSA, I can decompose the product into partial products and add them directly with ps and pc. This would merge the multiplier's compression tree with the accumulator's CSA, potentially reducing the total depth.

But this is complex to implement in RTL and Yosys might not optimize it well. Let me try a simpler approach: **reduce the CSA width to 16 bits** (matching the product width) and handle the sign extension at the output. The key insight is that bits 16-18 of ps and pc are always sign extensions of bit 15, so the CSA on those bits is trivial:
  -> create_file(content="// CSA with 16-bit data path, sign handled separately at output\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 16)  // product/CSA width\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  rst,\n    input\t\t  ld,\n    input\t\t  en,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,\n    input [PW-1:0]\t  ps_north,\n    input [PW-1:0]\t  pc_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,\n    output reg [PW-1:0]  ps_south,\n    output reg [PW-1:0]  pc_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // Product is 16 bits; no sign extension needed internally\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n   wire [PW-1:0] prod_lo = prod[PW-1:0];\n\n   // Full adder CSA on 16 bits\n   wire [PW-1:0] sum_out;\n   wire [PW-1:0] carry_out;\n   genvar k;\n   generate\n      for (k = 0; k < PW; k = k + 1) begin : fa\n         assign sum_out[k]   = ps_north[k] ^ pc_north[k] ^ prod_lo[k];\n         assign carry_out[k] = (ps_north[k] & pc_north[k]) |\n                               (ps_north[k] & prod_lo[k]) |\n                               (pc_north[k] & prod_lo[k]);\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) begin\n         w <= w_north;\n      end\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east   <= a_west;\n         ps_south <= sum_out;\n         pc_south <= {carry_out[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 16)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tld,\n    input\t\ten,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\n    output [N*PW-1:0]\tc_bot_s,\n    output [N*PW-1:0]\tc_bot_c\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]  a_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [PW-1:0]  ps_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [PW-1:0]  pc_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]  w_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign ps_v[0][j] = {PW{1'b0}};\n         assign pc_v[0][j] = {PW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]  = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot_s[j*PW +: PW] = ps_v[N][j];\n         assign c_bot_c[j*PW +: PW] = pc_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .ps_north   (ps_v[i][j]),\n               .pc_north   (pc_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .ps_south   (ps_v[i+1][j]),\n               .pc_south   (pc_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam INTW = 16;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*INTW-1:0] c_bot_s, c_bot_c;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(INTW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left),\n      .c_bot_s(c_bot_s), .c_bot_c(c_bot_c));\n\n   // CPA with sign extension from 16-bit to 19-bit equivalent then to 32-bit\n   // The carry-save values are 16-bit, but the true result needs sign extension\n   // cs + cc gives 16-bit result, sign-extend to 32\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [INTW-1:0] cs = c_bot_s[j*INTW +: INTW];\n         wire [INTW-1:0] cc = c_bot_c[j*INTW +: INTW];\n         wire [INTW-1:0] cpa = cs + cc;\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-INTW){cpa[INTW-1]}}, cpa};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [INTW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cpa;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-INTW){dr[D-1][INTW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v22.v')

## Summary

### Best Design: `design_v21.v` — Cost: 2,780,232 (down from baseline 4,624,409, a 40% reduction)

### What Worked Best (in order of impact)

1. **Carry-save accumulator (CSA) replacing carry-propagate adder** (v4→v8): The biggest single win. Each PE replaced its 32-bit ripple-carry adder with a 3:2 compressor (XOR sum + majority carry), deferring the final carry-propagate add to the array output. This cut delay from ~820ps to ~660ps. Area increased slightly due to dual sum/carry registers but net ADP dropped significantly.

2. **Narrowing accumulator from 32→19 bits** (v3): The theoretical minimum for 8×8 signed matmul (8 × max product 16384 = 131072 = 2^17, needs 19 bits signed). This reduced register and adder width across all 64 PEs, saving ~13% area and delay. 18 bits overflowed (confirmed by test failure at 131072).

3. **Removing reset from datapath registers** (v8): The `w`, `a_east`, `ps_south`, `pc_south` registers don't need reset — weight loading overwrites `w`, and the CSA chain is seeded with zeros from `psum_v[0]`. Only the `vpipe` (valid pipeline) needs reset for gate-level sim. This eliminated reset muxes, reducing both area and delay.

4. **Explicit full-adder generate loop** (v19→v21): Writing the CSA as an explicit `generate for` loop of full-adder cells instead of a single Boolean expression gave Yosys a different (better) synthesis mapping, trimming ~15ps from delay.

### What Didn't Work / Caused Regressions

- **Clearing psums during weight load** (v6): Broke mid-drain weight reloads — the original holds in-flight psums during stall, doesn't zero them.
- **Combinational `a_east` pass-through** (v12): Broke cycle-accurate timing — activations must register one hop per column.
- **Unsigned multiply + sign correction** (v17): The abs/negate logic added more delay than the signed multiply saved.
- **Carry-save in de-skew chain** (v9): Doubled de-skew registers (both sum+carry), area penalty exceeded delay savings.
- **16-bit CSA width** (v22): Sign extension wasn't handled correctly — carry out of bit 15 needed to propagate into sign bits.
- **Removing `target_delay` knob**: Lower (200-300ps) and higher (800-1200ps) values both produced worse ADP than default 500ps.

### Lessons Learned

- The CSA approach is clearly superior for systolic MAC chains — the carry-propagate adder depth (log₂(19) ≈ 5 stages) is eliminated from the per-cycle critical path.
- Minimum accumulator width matters: 19 bits is exact for this problem, confirmed by 18-bit overflow.
- RTL coding style affects Yosys synthesis results even for functionally identical logic — the explicit generate-loop FA produced a measurably better result than the inline Boolean CSA.
- Reset removal is a powerful area/delay optimization when the protocol guarantees registers are overwritten before use.
- Next time: I would explore explicit Wallace/Dadda tree merging of the multiplier partial products with the CSA to reduce the multiply-then-add serial depth, and try Booth recoding to halve the number of partial products.

================================================================================
